## [1.9.3] — 2026-05-21

### Fixed

- **Spec 0142 — Prompt capture for full-view modals** ([spec 0142](specs/0142-prompt-capture-for-full-view-modals.md)). On the anchor run `20260521-010637-dvs-backend-language-choice`, opening the Initial Brief full-view modal (`turnKey='input'` on the Phase 0 row of the timeline) renders an "agent default — the per-run system prompt for this older turn was not recorded" caveat instead of the recorded prompt. Three independent gaps combined: (1) the modal opened with the literal sentinel `'input'` and the server fell through to the spec-0085 synthesis path stamping `system_source: "agent-default"`; (2) `_derive_turn_key` collapsed Phase-0 round labels (`phase0-r{N}-{agent}`) to `phase0_<agent>` so successive round bundles overwrote each other; (3) Supabase carried zero `inputs/*` rows for the anchor run despite 33 files on disk (push-CLI gap tracked separately — see Known follow-ups). This patch fixes (1) and (2) on the persistence side; the modal now hydrates from a real recorded row on every new run.
  - **`inputs/input.json` persisted at session setup.** [`orchestrator/run.py`](src/dual_research/orchestrator/run.py) gains a `_persist_initial_brief_bundle` helper, called once after `brief.md` is read at the top of `run_session`. The file mirrors `aggregator.build_phase0_input_bundle()` — same 8-key `pieces` shape via `preflight_input_bundle(brief=brief_text, agent_name="<both>")`, same `agent="shared"` / `phase="phase0"` / `label="phase0-input"` envelope — but stamps `system_source: "recorded"` instead of `"agent-default"`. The frontend reads `system_source` to decide whether to render the "agent default" caveat; with the field set to `"recorded"` the caveat goes away. Idempotent: a resumed run that re-enters `run_session` finds the file already on disk and leaves it alone (no timestamp churn). The file is then picked up by the existing `_iter_file_rows` rglob and pushed to Supabase like every other input bundle, so the hosted Supabase-backed UI hydrates from the recorded row too.
  - **Phase-0 turn-keys are now round-keyed.** [`_call.py::_derive_turn_key`](src/dual_research/orchestrator/_call.py) and [`aggregator.py::_on_turn_inputs`](src/dual_research/ui/aggregator.py) (plus the matching site in `_on_turn_searches`) widened the round-key gate from `phase ∈ {2, 4}` to `phase ∈ {0, 2, 4}`. Pre-spec, four Phase-0 rounds collided on `inputs/phase0_<agent>.json` and only round 4 survived on disk; the timeline's four `phase0_round{1..4}_<agent>` cards all pointed at the same file. Post-spec, each Phase-0 round writes to `inputs/phase0_round{N}_<agent>.json` and the timeline cards land on real per-round bundles. Legacy single-shot Phase-0 transcripts whose labels carry no `-r{N}-` segment continue mapping to `phase0_<agent>` (idx == 0 → no round suffix), so pre-0114 fixtures and older runs keep working. Aligns the input/search persistence paths with the spec-0135 round-keying already in `_on_turn_ended`.
  - **No JSX changes.** [`run-detail.jsx`](src/dual_research/ui/static/run-detail.jsx) `InputBriefModal` and [`live-data.jsx`](src/dual_research/ui/static/live-data.jsx) timeline builder were already correct (the modal opens with `turnKey={item.turnKey || 'input'}`, the timeline stamps `turnKey: 'input'` on the brief card, and the per-round Phase-0 cards already used `phase0_round{R}_<agent>` keys); they were starved for backend data. The two server-side handlers ([`server.py::_read_input_bundle_fs`](src/dual_research/ui/server.py) and [`_read_input_bundle_supabase`](src/dual_research/ui/server.py)) already preferred a persisted bundle over the synth fallback for both `"input"` and round-keyed keys — they just never found anything to read. Now they do.
  - **Synth fallback retained for historic runs.** Spec §6 / §Risks: runs that pre-date this patch have no `inputs/input.json` row on disk or in Supabase. Their Initial Brief modal continues hydrating via the spec-0085 synth path with `system_source: "agent-default"` — the documented "older run" caveat the UI already renders. No backfill in this patch (deferred to a follow-up — re-deriving from each run's `brief.md` and pushing is feasible but separable).
  - **Open question resolved.** §9: chose the placeholder variant for `pieces.system` (current `preflight_input_bundle` behaviour with `_placeholder("brief")` substituted in the system body). Matches what the agent actually saw at call time — system was cached with the brief inlined via prompt caching — and stays consistent with the per-turn bundles already on disk. The literal-brief alternative was rejected as inconsistent.
  - **Tests.** New `tests/orchestrator/test_session_setup.py` — three cases pin the `_persist_initial_brief_bundle` helper: writes a file stamped `system_source="recorded"` with the brief round-tripped in `pieces.brief` and a non-empty `pieces.system`; is idempotent across re-invocations (resumed runs don't stomp the existing file); creates the `inputs/` subdir on a fresh session-dir. New `tests/orchestrator/test_call.py` — seven cases pin `_derive_turn_key`'s Phase-0 round-keying: rounds 1/2/3 produce `phase0_round{N}_<agent>` keys, openai → gpt alias preserved, no-round labels keep the legacy `phase0_<agent>` key, repair suffix preserved, Phase 1 still single-shot, Phase 2 regression-pinned. New aggregator test in `tests/ui/test_aggregator_input_bundles.py::test_phase0_round_keyed_inputs_survive_per_round` — two `turn_inputs` events at `phase0-r1-claude` and `phase0-r2-claude` land at distinct paths and neither overwrites the other; the pre-fix `phase0_claude.json` flat-key path is asserted absent. New server integration test in `tests/ui/test_server_input_bundles.py::test_persisted_input_json_overrides_synth_fallback` — when `inputs/input.json` is on disk, `GET /api/runs/<id>/inputs/input` returns the recorded payload (not the synthesised fallback) with `system_source: "recorded"` and the real prompt text. 1257 passed (up from 1245; +12 new tests).
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/orchestrator/run.py` (+`_persist_initial_brief_bundle` helper + invocation at session setup). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/_call.py` (Phase-0 in the round-key gate). `src/dual_research/ui/aggregator.py` (Phase-0 in both turn-input and turn-search round-key gates). `tests/orchestrator/test_session_setup.py` (new). `tests/orchestrator/test_call.py` (new). `tests/ui/test_aggregator_input_bundles.py` (+round-keyed-survives test). `tests/ui/test_server_input_bundles.py` (+persisted-overrides-synth test). `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` + `uv.lock` (`1.9.2 → 1.9.3`). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

## [1.9.2] — 2026-05-21

### Fixed

- **Spec 0141 — Critique aggregation invariants + resolved-view integrity** ([spec 0141](specs/0141-critique-aggregation-and-resolved-view-integrity.md)). Anchor run `20260521-010637-dvs-backend-language-choice` surfaced three data-integrity bugs sharing a single orchestrator-side root: B02 (`9 raised, 10 closed` — disagreement raise/close invariant violated), B06 (turns recording zero critique movement in Phase 0 / 2 / 4), B10 (Resolved view contradicts the Phase 4 timeline). Two surgical orchestrator fixes plus a derived-symptom verification close all three.
  - **B02 — Terminal-state guard on `AddressBlock`.** [`deep_research.py::apply_turn`](src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py) (the `AddressBlock` branch) gained an `is_terminal(ent.current_state)` short-circuit matching the existing pattern on `WithdrawBlock` (line 435) and `AcknowledgeBlock` (line 460). A late-arriving ADDRESS targeting an already-terminal item (resolved / acknowledged / withdrawn / capped) is silently dropped at the orchestrator and recorded as a new `ProtocolViolation` event for the audit trail. **Smoking gun pre-fix** on the anchor run, item `D-plan-g-01`: r2.1 claude ADDRESSED → r2.2 openai RESOLVED → r2.3 claude ADDRESSED the resolved item (leaks back to ADDRESSED) → r2.3 openai RESOLVED a second time. Post-fix the r2.3 claude ADDRESS is dropped, the item closes exactly once, and the UI aggregator's `closed > raised` invariant violation goes away.
  - **B06 — `EmptyTurnDetected` signal scoped to phases 0 / 2 / 4.** A new event fires when a parsed turn carries zero ledger-affecting blocks (no RAISE / ADDRESS / RESOLVE / WITHDRAW / ACKNOWLEDGE) in a negotiate / review phase. Phases 1 (parallel drafts) and 3 (single-agent drafting) are by design item-silent and skip the check. The event carries `finish_reason` and `output_tokens` plumbed via keyword arguments from the upstream `turn_ended` payload into `apply_turn`, so consumers can distinguish "model hit `max_tokens` mid-output" (the dispositive Phase 4 r6 claude shape: `finish_reason='max_tokens', output_tokens=8750`) from a genuinely empty model output. Informational only — does not abort the turn or retry; a future spec can lean on the signal for prompt-tightening or re-prompt policy.
  - **B10 — Resolved view consistency verified by anchor-run replay.** With the B02 leak plugged, items can only close once, so the `item.closedRound` projection in [`ui/items.py`](src/dual_research/ui/items.py) (and `ui/aggregator.py`'s `closed_turn_key`) reads the single terminal transition deterministically. Replaying `20260521-010637-dvs-backend-language-choice` against the patched orchestrator confirms terminal-transition counts equal raise counts per kind (`disagreement: 15 / 15` post-fix, was `15 / 16`). No UI / JSX changes in this spec; the existing Resolved-group renderer at [`run-detail.jsx:6576`](src/dual_research/ui/static/run-detail.jsx) produces the right view once the upstream events are clean.
  - **New event types.** `ProtocolViolation` (orchestrator dropped a block that would have violated an item-state invariant; today: `violation_code='terminal_state_re_address'`). `EmptyTurnDetected` (negotiate / review turn produced zero ledger-affecting blocks). Both follow the existing `CloseoutViolation` shape and ride the spec-0122 transcript bridge — added to `_TRANSCRIPT_MIRRORED_EVENTS` in [`orchestrator/run.py`](src/dual_research/orchestrator/run.py) so they persist to `transcript.jsonl` alongside the existing lifecycle events.
  - **Plumbing.** `apply_turn`'s return tuple widened from 3 → 4 lists (raised / transitions / violations / empty-turn events); `violations` now carries `CloseoutViolation | ProtocolViolation`. `RoundResult` gained `empty_turn_events: tuple[EmptyTurnDetected, ...]`. `process_round_end` accepts the new list (back-compat default of `()`). All three call sites updated: production (`dr_run.py::_drive_interaction_phase`) extracts `finish_reason` / `output_tokens` from `result.extras` / `result.usage`; sync test driver (`deep_research.py::run_round`); replay (`ledger/replay.py`) passes `None` / `0` since the on-disk path can't see the upstream payload.
  - **Tests.** New unit tests in [`tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py`](tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py): terminal-state guard rejects `resolved → addressed`, `acknowledged → addressed`, `withdrawn → addressed`, `capped → addressed` (parameterised) and emits one `ProtocolViolation` each with `violation_code='terminal_state_re_address'`; `open → addressed` and `addressed → resolved` happy paths unchanged; `addressed → addressed` no-op still short-circuits silently (no violation). New `EmptyTurnDetected` tests: fires in phases 0 / 2 / 4 only; carries `finish_reason` + `output_tokens` from kwargs; does NOT fire when any ledger-affecting block is present. New replay test against `runs/20260521-010637-dvs-backend-language-choice/`: per-kind terminal-transition count equals raise count (`disagreement: 15 / 15`); at least one `ProtocolViolation` recorded (the seq-137 re-open); at least one `EmptyTurnDetected` recorded for the Phase 4 r6 claude turn. Test (skipped if the run directory is absent so CI on fresh clones stays green.)
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/events/types.py` (+`ProtocolViolation`, +`EmptyTurnDetected`). `src/dual_research/events/__init__.py` (exports). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py` (terminal-state guard + empty-turn detector + `apply_turn` signature + `RoundResult.empty_turn_events` + `process_round_end` + sync `run_round`). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py` (production `apply_turn` callsite + `_publish_round_events`). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/run.py` (`_TRANSCRIPT_MIRRORED_EVENTS`). `src/dual_research/ledger/replay.py` (replay callsite). `tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py` + new `tests/ledger/test_replay_spec_0141.py`. `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` + `uv.lock` (`1.9.1 → 1.9.2`). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

## [1.9.1] — 2026-05-21

### Fixed

- **Spec 0140 — Phase 4 deadlock: draft extractor body retention + escape-valve breadth** ([spec 0140](specs/0140-phase4-deadlock-extractor-and-escape-valve.md)). Anchor run `20260521-010637-dvs-backend-language-choice` burned the full 8-round Phase 4 hard cap and exited with `via_hard_cap=true`, leaving the final draft on disk as a 76-byte stub despite ~$10 of LLM spend. One causal chain — **B12 → B13 → B07** — produced the deadlock and is fixed in three surgical edits.
  - **B12 — Draft extractor truncated at body sub-headings.** The Phase 4 drafter-revision call site in [dr_run.py](src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py) was using the strict `extract_revised_draft`, which terminates the body at the first `^##\s+\S` heading outside a fenced code block. On `round-07-claude.md` the drafter opened `## Revised draft` then wrote `## 1. Executive Summary`, `## 2. Version Baseline`, `## 3. Tier 1 Pass/Fail`, `## 4. Ranked Candidates` as sibling sub-sections; the extractor captured only the 7-line preamble. **Fix:** switch the call site to `extract_revised_draft_inclusive` and extend its `_PROTOCOL_TOP_HEADINGS` allowlist with the Spec 0114 v2 sentinels (`Stance`, `Addressing items raised against me`, `Ratifying my own items`, `New items I'm raising`, `Phase artifact`, `Status`, `Closeout constraints`). The inclusive extractor now absorbs any `##` heading that is NOT a protocol sentinel and terminates at the first one that is — so `## 1. Executive Summary` etc. are retained as part of the draft body, and the on-disk `draft-vN.md` carries the full text.
  - **B13 — Escape valve too narrow.** Spec 0137's substantive-convergence escape valve at [dr_run.py::_drive_interaction_phase](src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py) (mirrored in [deep_research.py::DeepResearchPhase.run](src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py)) required both agents to emit `AGREED` simultaneously. On the anchor run claude went AGREED but openai stayed IN_PROGRESS, because the reviewer was looking at a 76-byte stub draft and could not honestly ratify nothing. **Fix:** widen the precondition to fire on `one-agent AGREED + terminal ledger + round_no >= caps.soft` in addition to the both-AGREED case. The soft-cap gate (4 for phase 4) ensures both agents had a full round budget to surface items before the branch arms. The transcript line carries a new `trigger` discriminator (`both_agreed` / `one_agreed_terminal`) so replay can tell the two paths apart; the published `ArtifactCanonicallyPromoted` event schema is unchanged.
  - **B07 — Hard-cap deadlock** falls out automatically: with the extractor retaining full draft bodies and the escape valve catching the one-agent-AGREED late-round shape, the run terminates at natural convergence instead of burning to `caps_for(4).hard == 8`.
  - **Tests.** New unit tests in `tests/protocol/test_parse.py` cover the anchor-run shape (`## 1. … ## 4.` sub-sections retained), each Spec 0114 sentinel as a terminator, the grep regression (`dr_run.py` no longer imports the strict extractor), and an anchor-run replay (`runs/.../round-07-claude.md` → body >= 25k chars including `## 4. Ranked Candidates`). New unit tests in `tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py` cover the widened escape valve at and below the soft cap. The pre-existing Spec 0137 both-AGREED + hash-drift test is unchanged. Full suite: **1228 passed.**
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py` (extractor swap + escape-valve widening). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py` (mirror widening in the sync path). `src/dual_research/protocol/parse.py` (extend `_PROTOCOL_TOP_HEADINGS`). `tests/protocol/test_parse.py` + `tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py`. `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.9.0 → 1.9.1`). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

## [1.9.0] — 2026-05-21

### Added

- **Spec 0138 — Active-agent gradient pulse, uniform critique card heights, run-id chip in header row 2** ([spec 0138](specs/0138-active-agent-pulse-critique-card-parity-and-run-id-chip.md)). Three coordinated run-detail surface changes. **(1) Active-agent badges now pulse.** The two `.as.in-header` AgentStrip badges in the Timeline pane (Claude in `.tl__head`, GPT in `.tl__tabs`, placed by spec 0133) previously signalled "this model is doing work" only through a 6 px dot in their right cluster — easy to miss in a crowded pane. A five-variant prototype study at [`prototypes/active-agent-pulse/mockup.html`](prototypes/active-agent-pulse/mockup.html) (soft halo, gradient sweep, elevation breathing, border-tint glow, combined) converged on Variant B — a gentle agent-tinted soft-light gradient that sweeps right-to-left across the badge surface over 3.2 s. 18% alpha against the surface-container background; the sweep is felt before it's seen and never competes with the inner dot pulse. Both agents can be live simultaneously; the GPT badge carries `animation-delay: -1.6s` so they don't lock-step. Under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, the live state degrades to a static `surface-container-high` tint so liveness stays legible without motion. New `--agent-{a,b}-rgb` triples in `tokens.css` carry the alpha-friendly palette. **(2) Critique card height parity.** Every critique-pane `QuestionThread` previously rendered a small "id: D-plan-c-01" line under the chip header — always present, even when collapsed — which combined with the parent `.qthread`'s `gap: 12px` added ~28 px to every card relative to the matching timeline turn card. The id was fully redundant (the `D`/`Q`/`I`/`C` prefix duplicates the category chip, the `-c-`/`-g-` suffix duplicates the provider icon chip, the round counter duplicates the "raised in r{N}" chip; the phase fragment is disambiguated by the surrounding `.crit-group` header). Dropping the line lets every collapsed critique card match the vertical footprint of a collapsed timeline turn card. The expanded card's gap behaviour (header → lifecycle body separator) is unchanged. **(3) Run-id chip in `RunDetailHeader` row 2.** Adds a `RunIDChip` rendering the full `run.id` to the right-side cluster of `PhaseDotsRow`, immediately left of the started / elapsed / round metadata. Click copies the id to the clipboard; the chip's `title` swaps to "copied!" for 1.4 s as confirmation. Uses the existing `.rid` chip primitive — no new design-system surface. The 4-char hex prefix in the run-list page is unchanged; this is an additional affordance for referring to the run elsewhere.
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/ui/static/tokens.css` (new `--agent-{a,b}-rgb` triples). `src/dual_research/ui/static/components.css` (Variant B gradient-sweep recipe on `.as.in-header.is-live::before`, reduced-motion fallback, `.crit-card-id` rule deleted). `src/dual_research/ui/static/run-detail.jsx` (`TimelineAgentPill` threads `is-live` through its `className`; `PhaseDotsRow` mounts the new `RunIDChip` with copy-to-clipboard handler). `src/dual_research/ui/static/shared.jsx` (`QuestionThread` no longer renders the `.crit-card-id` div). `src/dual_research/ui/static/index.html` (cache-bust `?v=0136b → ?v=0138a`). `specs/0138-…md` (new). `prototypes/active-agent-pulse/{mockup.html,README.md}` (new). `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.8.3 → 1.9.0`). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

## [1.8.3] — 2026-05-21

### Fixed

- **Spec 0137 — Substantive-convergence escape valve for hash-drift dead-loops** ([spec 0137](specs/0137-substantive-convergence-escape-valve.md)). Production run `81cc` (`20260520-170146-dvs-backend-language-choice`) reproduced an issue the user has been chasing since the legacy → Deep Research cutover: in Phase 2, by round 5 every ledger item was terminal (12 questions / disagreements all resolved or withdrawn), in rounds 6 / 7 / 8 both agents emitted `STATUS: AGREED` with matching `DRAFTER: claude`, and **no new items were raised** — but the phase exited at hard cap with `converged=false drafter=null`. Three rounds of LLM spend (~$1.50, 70+ seconds per round) produced zero substantive content; the agents kept paraphrasing their own `AGREED_PLAN` bodies (claude's ~19 k normalised chars, openai's ~14 k, hashes thrashing round-over-round) and never landed byte-identical text. The same shape was observable in Phase 0 round 4. **Root cause.** The Deep Research three-gate convergence check ([orchestrator/closeout.py::check_convergence](src/dual_research/orchestrator/closeout.py)) requires (i) both `STATUS: AGREED`, (ii) zero non-terminal items, AND (iii) byte-equal artifact hashes. The legacy `phase2.py` carried a Spec 0032 hash-drift escape valve (force-verbatim repair turn → canonical-promote on second drift) for exactly this case; the Spec 0114 cutover to `dr_run.py::_drive_interaction_phase` did **not** port it. The closeout mechanism handles "both AGREED + items non-terminal", hard-cap handles "ran out of rounds", but the "both AGREED + items terminal + hash drift" gap had no handler — the agents iterated until hard cap doing nothing.
  - **The escape valve** ([dr_run.py::_drive_interaction_phase](src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py) + [deep_research.py::DeepResearchPhase.run](src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py)). After `phase.process_round_end(...)` returns `rr` and before the closeout / hard-cap branches: if both agents emit `AGREED` and `items_blocking_convergence` is empty but `rr.converged` is False, the artifact-hash gate is the only remaining blocker. Promote the canonical artifact from the designated agent's turn (claude for phase 0; the drafter for phase 2 / 4), set `via_artifact_promotion = True`, emit `ArtifactCanonicallyPromoted`, and break. No repair turn — the downstream `run_dr_phase{0,2,4}` post-loop code already reads the canonical artifact from one specific agent's turn file regardless, so the non-canonical agent's drift was never load-bearing. **One branch, no extra LLM call.**
  - **Why no force-verbatim repair turn?** A repair turn was the legacy fix's first stage — spend one more LLM call to get the non-drafter to copy the drafter's text. But the text only matters to the orchestrator's hash check; nothing downstream consumes the non-drafter's emission. Skipping the repair saves cost and rounds without changing the downstream artifact.
  - **Telemetry.** New `ArtifactCanonicallyPromoted` event (`phase`, `round`). New `PhaseConverged.via_artifact_promotion: bool` field — the four `via_*` flags now form an exhaustive partition of non-organic convergence paths. New `PhaseRunResult.via_artifact_promotion: bool` threads through both the sync `DeepResearchPhase.run` path and the async `_drive_interaction_phase` path so the UI can distinguish "agents substantively agreed" from "agents hit the ceiling" in future renders.
  - **No prompt changes.** The valve fires at the end of the first round where both go AGREED with a terminal ledger, so subsequent rounds — which would have been the natural place to surface a "you're blocked on hash" warning — never happen. Existing verbatim-copy prompt guidance stays in place for the common path where one agent does adopt the other's text organically.
  - **Tests.** Four new tests in [`tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py`](tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py): valve fires when both AGREED + ledger terminal + hash drifts; valve does **not** fire when one item is still non-terminal (closeout must take precedence); valve does **not** fire when only one agent AGREED (loop continues / hard-cap fires); organic byte-equal convergence keeps every `via_*` flag at False. Full suite: **1222 passed**.
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py` (new branch + import). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py` (`PhaseRunResult.via_artifact_promotion`, `build_phase_converged_event`, mirror branch in `DeepResearchPhase.run`). `src/dual_research/events/types.py` (`ArtifactCanonicallyPromoted`, `PhaseConverged.via_artifact_promotion`). `src/dual_research/events/__init__.py` (export). `tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py` (4 new tests). `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.8.2 → 1.8.3`). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).

## [1.8.2] — 2026-05-21

### Fixed

- **Spec 0136 follow-up — Supabase list path + non-zero exit-code generalisation.** The v1.8.1 spec-0136 fix unified the status truth table for the disk-backed paths (`summarize_run` + `load_run_snapshot`) and verified locally that DVS-backend / pv-backend-language / LLM-vs-human-grading all flipped from `running`/`completed` → `deadlocked`. But the user immediately surfaced the same divergence on the **hosted** surface — and rightly so: there was a third status deriver, `_status_from_columns` in [`server.py:1075`](src/dual_research/ui/server.py), that maps the Supabase `runs` table columns onto the UI status enum for the All-Runs list when the server runs in Supabase mode. That helper called `derive_run_status` but flattened `exit_code` into derived `run_failed` / `hard_cap_hit` booleans **before** the truth table saw it, which discarded the `exit_code == 0` + not-done signal and left the hosted list reading `running` indefinitely for any pushed run that exited cleanly without reaching `done` — exactly the DVS-backend / LLM-vs-human-grading pattern. The detail-page path on hosted was correct (it materialises the session through `load_run_snapshot`, which already had the fix); only the list path was wrong.
  - **Pass exit_code through.** `_status_from_columns` now passes `run_completed_exit_code=exit_code` to `derive_run_status`. The truth table's silent-exit defence branch (`exit_code == 0` + not done → `deadlocked`) fires for Supabase-backed rows the same way it fires for disk-backed sessions.
  - **Generalise the errored predicate.** `derive_run_status` previously hard-coded the errored exit codes as `{2, 52}` (EXIT_RUNTIME + EXIT_PROTOCOL_PARSE_FAILURE). Pre-spec the disk-backed `_on_run_completed` handler treated `{1, 2, 52}` as errored; the legacy hosted `_status_from_columns` treated *any* non-`{None, 0, 51}` exit code as failed. Both meanings collapsed onto a tighter rule: any non-zero exit code that isn't the dedicated hard-cap signal (51) → `errored`. Catches exit 1 (Python uncaught exception), exit 137 (SIGKILL), exit 143 (SIGTERM), etc. — anything the orchestrator didn't structure.
  - **Tests.** New `TestSupabaseListStatusHelper` class in `test_aggregator_status.py` exercises the exact production payload shape (`phase_reached='phase2', exit_code=0, state.final_emitted_to=null` → `deadlocked`; same for hard-cap / runtime-error / in-flight). New truth-table test `test_unknown_nonzero_exit_code_is_errored` covers exit codes 1 / 3 / 137 / 143. Full suite: **1218 passed**.
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/ui/server.py` (`_status_from_columns` rewrite). `src/dual_research/ui/labels.py` (`derive_run_status` errored generalisation). `src/dual_research/ui/static/index.html` (cache-bust `?v=0136a → ?v=0136b`). `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.8.1 → 1.8.2`). `tests/ui/test_aggregator_status.py` (new test class + 1 new truth-table case). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).
  - **Verification.** Hosted DVS-backend + LLM-vs-human-grading list rows now read `deadlocked` consistently with their detail-page status.

## [1.8.1] — 2026-05-21

### Fixed

- **Spec 0136 — Unify run-status derivation across the All-Runs list, the run-detail page, and the orchestrator's exit-code emission** ([spec 0136](specs/0136-unify-run-status-derivation.md)). Two independent live runs surfaced the same symptom on the same day: All-Runs list said `running` while the run-detail page said `completed`, even though the run had silently exited at the Phase 2 hard cap without converging. Truthfully it was **deadlocked**, but neither surface said so. The detail-page timeline also dropped the last round's output card — a downstream symptom of the wrong terminal status. Three layered bugs: (1) the orchestrator's shared `_drive_interaction_phase` driver gated `HardCapHit` emission on `hard_cap_remaining_items` being non-empty, so the "every item terminal but no AGREED status" pattern silently exited with `exit_code 0`; (2) the All-Runs path's `_scan_terminal_signals` didn't read `run_completed` at all, so its `derive_run_status` fall-through swallowed any "exited cleanly without reaching done" case as `running` forever; (3) the run-detail path's `_on_run_completed` mapped `exit_code 0 → "completed"` blindly without cross-checking `state.phase` or `final_emitted_to`. **Three coordinated changes.**
  - **Orchestrator predicate** ([dr_run.py:336-345](src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py) + [deep_research.py:827-834](src/dual_research/orchestrator/deep_research.py)). Drop the `if hard:` / `if hard_caps:` gating predicate on both the async (`_drive_interaction_phase`) and sync (`DeepResearchPhase.run`) paths. `HardCapHit` + `via_hard_cap = True` now fire whenever the loop reaches `caps.hard` without convergence, regardless of remaining-item count. Cascades through `phase2_outcome.hard_capped → exit_code = EXIT_HARD_CAP (51) → "deadlocked"` correctly for new runs; legacy transcripts are caught by the truth-table defence branch (below).
  - **Unified status truth table** ([labels.py::derive_run_status](src/dual_research/ui/labels.py)). Single function consumed by both `summarize_run` (All-Runs list, state.json + transcript-tail scan) and `load_run_snapshot` (run-detail page, transcript replay). New parameter `run_completed_exit_code`; new 6-row precedence:
    1. `run_failed` → `errored`
    2. `exit_code ∈ {2, 52}` (EXIT_RUNTIME, EXIT_PROTOCOL_PARSE_FAILURE) → `errored`
    3. `hard_cap_hit` OR `exit_code == 51` (EXIT_HARD_CAP) → `deadlocked`
    4. `final_emitted` OR `state.phase == "done"` → `completed`
    5. `exit_code == 0` AND not (final_emitted OR done) → `deadlocked` *(silent-exit defence — covers legacy on-disk transcripts produced before the predicate fix)*
    6. else → `running`
  - **List-page scanner** ([aggregator.py::_scan_terminal_signals](src/dual_research/ui/aggregator.py)). Return tuple extended from `(hard_cap_hit, run_failed)` to `(hard_cap_hit, run_failed, run_completed_exit_code)`. `summarize_run` passes the new value through to `derive_run_status`.
  - **Detail-page replay** ([aggregator.py::_on_run_completed / _on_run_failed / _on_final_emitted / hard_cap_hit dispatch](src/dual_research/ui/aggregator.py)). Imperative `run.status = …` assignments replaced with a stash-and-recompute pattern: each handler records its terminal signal on a private `Run._terminal_signals` field (new `TerminalSignals` dataclass); a `_finalise_status` helper runs `derive_run_status` once after every `apply_event` tick (and once more after `_augment_from_state_json` at the end of `load_run_snapshot`). The `_terminal_signals` field is stripped at the JSON boundary — `to_jsonable` now drops keys starting with `_` so internal book-keeping stays off the wire.
  - **Result.** Both UI surfaces now agree. On the live local server (post-fix): DVS-backend run flipped from `running`/`completed` → `deadlocked` on both surfaces; pv-backend-language similarly corrected from `completed` → `deadlocked` (same silent-exit pattern). Healthy completed runs continue to read `completed`; in-flight runs continue to read `running`. The four-value status enum (`running` / `completed` / `deadlocked` / `errored`) is unchanged on the wire.
  - **Tests.** New [`tests/ui/test_aggregator_status.py`](tests/ui/test_aggregator_status.py) — 7 truth-table scenarios + 6 path-agreement integration tests + the on-disk DVS regression. New sync-path orchestrator test in [`test_deep_research.py`](tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py) for hard-cap-with-no-remaining-items. Updated existing `test_run_completed_exit_codes` to reflect the new truth table (exit_code 0 without done → `deadlocked` via silent-exit defence; exit_code 0 + final-emitted → `completed`). Full suite: **1212 passed**.
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py` + `orchestrator/deep_research.py` (predicate fix on both paths). `src/dual_research/ui/labels.py` (truth table). `src/dual_research/ui/aggregator.py` (`_scan_terminal_signals`, dispatch, handlers, `_finalise_status`, `load_run_snapshot` hook). `src/dual_research/ui/models.py` (`Run._terminal_signals` field, `TerminalSignals` dataclass, `to_jsonable` underscore-key strip). `tests/ui/test_aggregator_status.py` (new) + `tests/ui/test_aggregator.py` (updated) + `tests/orchestrator/test_deep_research.py` (new test). `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.8.0 → 1.8.1` PATCH bump). `src/dual_research/ui/static/index.html` (cache-bust `?v=0135a → ?v=0136a` on 25 references). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).
  - **Out of scope.** Broadening convergence semantics (whether "every item terminal" should count as success); renaming `deadlocked` for the silent-exit case; process-level liveness detection (heartbeat for OOM-killed local runs); per-phase status badges.

## [1.8.0] — 2026-05-21

### Added

- **Spec 0135 — Phase 0 round cards + side-by-side critique modal** ([spec 0135](specs/0135-phase0-round-cards-and-side-by-side-critique.md)). Spec 0114 made Phase 0 a full multi-round brief negotiation under the Deep Research protocol — the same `_drive_interaction_phase` coroutine that drives Phase 2 (plan negotiation) and Phase 4 (draft review) — but nine downstream wiring gaps prevented the round-by-round surface from showing. The timeline collapsed Phase 0 back into two single-shot "preflight" cards regardless of how many rounds the agents actually negotiated; the full-view modal opened a single-pane response viewer instead of the side-by-side critique pane used everywhere else; review items, ledgers, summaries, token usage, and live-streaming bodies were all gated to phases 2 and 4. This spec closes every gap with surgical, structurally-mirrored changes — no protocol churn, no new prompts, no new on-disk artefacts.
  - **`Phase0RoundComplete` event.** New marker event in `events/types.py`, exported from `events/__init__.py`, emitted from `dr_run._publish_legacy_round_complete` once per Phase 0 round (matching Phase 2's / Phase 4's per-round granularity). Counter fields default to `None` per the spec-0115 convention — per-category data flows via `ItemRaised` / `ItemTransitioned`.
  - **`PhaseStats.phase0` becomes round-keyed.** Type annotation in `ui/models.py` shifts from `dict[str, TurnStats]` to `dict` (dual-shape during the transition window). New-protocol runs land in the round-keyed shape `{1: {claude, gpt}, 2: {…}, …}` mirroring `phase2` / `phase4`; legacy pre-0114 transcripts (only `phase0/preflight-{agent}.md` on disk) keep the per-agent `{claude, gpt}` shape. `turn_stats.build_phase_stats` picks the right shape based on disk state — preferring round files when present, falling back to preflight files otherwise. New helper `_phase0_round_stats` parses `STATUS` + `OPEN_QUESTIONS` + `BLOCKING_DISAGREEMENTS` + `FINAL_SURFACED_DISAGREEMENTS` + `BRIEF_ISSUES` markers from each Phase 0 round file.
  - **Review items + prior blocks extend to Phase 0.** `aggregator._read_phase_review_items` loops over `(0, 2, 4)` instead of `(2, 4)`; `_resolve_prior_blocks` gets a `phase_n == 0` branch (round 1 → `brief.md`; round ≥ 2 → other agent's prior Phase 0 turn). Bucket keys become `phase0_round{R}_<agent>` matching the Phase 2 / Phase 4 convention.
  - **Phase 0 ledger projection.** `item_projection.project_phase_ledgers` returns `{0, 2, 4}` instead of `{2, 4}` — the chip-deltas and cross-round ledger math for Phase 0 land in `run.phaseLedgers[0]` automatically. Phase 0 items in `bundle.items` already projected correctly through `_item_to_ledger_entry` (the helper was never phase-gated).
  - **Phase-summary + token-usage + live-body wiring (G7 / G8 / G9).** `_read_phase_summaries` extends its round-keyed loop to `(0, 2, 4)` — collapsed Phase 0 round cards now pick up each turn's `## Summary` TL;DR. `_on_turn_ended` token-usage keying widens to `phase_int in (0, 2, 4)` — the Consumption tab now shows one card per `(round, agent)` for Phase 0 instead of collapsing all rounds into a single per-agent card. `_populate_current_bodies` for `run.phase == 0` reads `phase0/round-NN-{agent}.md` when round files exist (latest round → in-flight live card body), falling back to `preflight-{agent}.md` for legacy transcripts.
  - **Aggregator dispatch.** `apply_event` accepts `phase0_round_complete` alongside `phase2_round_complete` / `phase4_round_complete` — the existing `_on_round_complete` handler advances `run.round.current` uniformly across all three phases.
  - **Frontend timeline.** `live-data.jsx::buildLiveTimeline` Phase 0 branch enumerates `1..N` round × agent cards (matching the Phase 2 / Phase 4 pattern: completed `turn` cards + a `turn-live` pair for the in-flight round). Cards are stamped with `statsPhase: 0` so `attachItemStats` routes lookups to `phaseStats.phase0[round][agent]`. The legacy two-`preflight`-card fallback fires when no integer keys are present in `phaseStats.phase0` (pre-0114 transcripts). The phase-divider `extra` label reads `${N} round${s}` like Phase 2 / Phase 4. Brief "input" card unchanged; sits leftmost in the Phase 0 section.
  - **Frontend modal routing.** `ArtifactModal` extends the `NegotiateReviewModal` route to `statsPhase ∈ {0, 2, 4}`. `priorContentPathFor` adds the Phase 0 case (round 1 → `brief.md`; round ≥ 2 → other agent's prior phase-0 turn). `leftPaneTabsFor` adds the Phase 0 branch with `[Other's prior turn]` (R ≥ 2 only) + `[Brief]` (always). `reviewItemsFor` was already key-generic and picks up `phase0Round{R}{Agent}` buckets automatically. Subtitle reads *"critiquing the brief"* (R1) or *"responding to {other}'s prior critique"* (R ≥ 2).
  - **Frontend composers.** `composeGist` + `composeSentiment` get explicit Phase 0 multi-round branches (mirroring the Phase 2 sentence builders) — round 1 reads as *"Cautious — {agent}'s round-1 brief critique"*; later rounds compose from question/disagreement movements just like Phase 2 turns. Legacy `kind === 'preflight'` branches stay intact for the pre-0114 fallback.
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/events/types.py` + `events/__init__.py` (new event class + export). `src/dual_research/orchestrator/dr_run.py` (emit + import). `src/dual_research/ui/models.py` (dual-shape annotation). `src/dual_research/ui/turn_stats.py` (build_phase_stats + new helper). `src/dual_research/ui/aggregator.py` (dispatch tuple, review-items loop, prior-blocks branch, phase-summaries loop, token-usage keying, live-body branch, carry-forward shape guard). `src/dual_research/ui/item_projection.py` (phase-0 in the ledger output). `src/dual_research/ui/static/live-data.jsx` (timeline builder + `attachItemStats`). `src/dual_research/ui/static/run-detail.jsx` (modal route, `priorContentPathFor`, `leftPaneTabsFor`, gist/sentiment, subtitle). `tests/ui/test_item_projection.py` + `tests/ui/test_turn_stats.py` (new + updated tests). `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.7.1 → 1.8.0` MINOR bump). `CHANGELOG.md` (this entry).
  - **Out of scope.** Legacy `PreflightResponseModal` codepath stays as the fallback for pre-0114 transcripts — retiring it is a deletion-only follow-up once hosted-deployment transcripts confirm zero pre-0114 runs remain. The brief "input" card is unchanged. No protocol / contract / on-disk schema changes.

## [1.7.1] — 2026-05-20

### Changed

- **Spec 0133 follow-up — bump narrow-mode breakpoint 1499 → 1799 px.** Post-deploy validation surfaced that the spec-0133 narrow-mode rules weren't kicking in for the most common laptop viewports. The original breakpoint (`max-width: 1499px`) was inherited from SPEC-0125's filter-row rule, but the spec-0133 block grew to govern much more (relocated agent pills + Bar 1 compaction + Bar 2 status/agent label collapse + segmented control icons-only). The combined wide-mode content (Timeline pill + Conversation/Consumption tabs in one row; Critique title + three phase tabs + three counter triples in another; ten filter chips with full labels in a third) needs each two-pane column at ≥ ~900 px to fit, which requires viewport ≥ ~1800 px. MacBook Pro 16" (1728 px), MacBook Pro 14" (1512 px), and the typical half-screen browser window on a 27" display all sit in the 1500–1799 range and were missing narrow rules entirely. Bumping the shared block at `components.css:763` from `1499 → 1799 px` covers them. The unrelated `.agent-input` (`components.css:1072`) and `.resp-grid` (`components.css:2987`) breakpoints stay at 1499 — different layouts, different threshold.
  - **Cache-bust** `?v=0133c → ?v=0133d` on every `<link>` / `<script>` in `index.html` (25 occurrences).
  - **Version bump** 1.7.0 → 1.7.1 (PATCH — visual threshold adjustment, no behavior change).

## [1.7.0] — 2026-05-20

### Added

- **Spec 0133 — Run-detail surface rework: agent chips into Timeline pane + narrow critique compaction + M3 segmented phase progress + timeline card chip slim-down** ([spec 0133](specs/0133-run-detail-header-rework.md)). Frontend-only restructure of the run-detail page surface (spec 0107 chrome + spec 0119 / 0124 card heads). Eight coordinated changes in `run-detail.jsx`, `shared.jsx`, and `components.css`; no protocol / contract / backend churn.
  - **Agent chips into the Timeline pane headers.** `<TimelineAgentBar>` (the dedicated `.agent-bar` row that sat between `RunDetailHeader` and the two-pane grid) is removed. The Claude `TimelineAgentPill` now renders inside `<header className="tl__head">` and the GPT one inside `<div className="tl__tabs">`, both right-aligned via `margin-left: auto` on the new `.as.in-header` modifier. Restores the placement intent originally documented in `components.css:283-294` (`.as-timeline` comment: *"Claude on PaneHeader row, GPT on PaneToolbar row"*) that spec 0105 had folded into a shared agent-bar parent.
  - **Wide-view chip content.** Each pill renders `[logo] [model] · [tokens] · [cost-2-decimals] · [● activity phrase]`. Agent name ("Claude" / "GPT") dropped — logo + model carry identity. Cost down from 4 decimals (`$0.7099`) to 2 (`$0.71`) via the existing `fmt.costShort` helper, plumbed through `<AgentStrip>` via a new optional `costFormatter` prop that defaults to `fmt.cost` (every other AgentStrip consumer stays at 4 decimals). `min-width: 600px` sized to fit the longest live activity phrase (22 chars — `drafting parallel plan` / `drafting converged doc` / `negotiating · round NN`) without truncation; both chips share the min-width so they render at identical outer widths despite the model-name length difference.
  - **Narrow-view chip content (≤ 1499 px).** Tokens + cost + their separators drop (read at the run-total level via `CostBadge` in the top bar); the pill becomes `[logo] [model] · [● activity]`. Both chips lock to `width: 380px` so they stay visually equal-sized despite "gpt-5.5" being much shorter than "claude-sonnet-4-6". The Conversation / Consumption segmented control collapses to icons-only at the same breakpoint (`font-size: 0` collapses the text node next to the `.ms` icon span; the icon span re-asserts its own font-size, so no HTML edit is needed) to make room for the wider pill alongside it. Row right-padding tightens from `20px` → `10px`.
  - **Critique Bar 1 narrow compaction.** Force `flex-wrap: nowrap` on `.crit2 .bar1` and `.crit2 .right` so the title + phase tabs + counter cluster can't wrap to a second row at narrow widths (previously broke the height match with `.tl__head` across the centre divider). Drop `.phase-tab .pname` (keep just `P2` / `P4` / `Σ` codes) and `.crit-totals .lbl` (keep just the three numbers). Bar 1 stays a single row at the same `min-height` as `.tl__head`.
  - **Critique Bar 2 narrow compaction.** Extend SPEC-0125's kind-filter label collapse to the status filter cluster (Open / Resolved / Drift — identified by `.chip-dot`) and the agent filter cluster (Claude / GPT — identified by `.chip-leading-icon`) via `:has()`. The dot color + agent icon already carry the identity at this width; the text is redundant. The whole filter row stays single-line.
  - **M3 segmented linear phase-progress indicator.** Replaces the legacy `<PhaseDots>` circles-and-lines treatment (composed entirely via inline `style={{ … }}` props — pre-M3 anti-pattern that survived the 0128 token sweep because its values were geometric, not v1 tokens). New `.phase-progress` element renders one `.phase-progress__seg` cell per `PHASES` entry; each cell is a 26 × 4 px rounded bar with a 3 px gap between cells. Cell state class drives background: `--md-outline-variant` (track / pending), `--p-ok` (done), `--p-info` (current), `--p-err` (errored), `--p-warn` (deadlocked). Transition uses `--md-dur-short-4` / `--md-easing-standard` (M3 motion vocabulary, not the deprecated `--m-base` / `--ease`).
  - **Timeline turn-card category-chip slim-down.** In every `TlTurnRow` card across every phase, the per-round Q / D / I / C chips drop their `categoryBubble` (bubble glyph) and `value` (standing total) slots. Each chip becomes a slim Δ-pair: `+raised | −closed` only. Category identity is carried by tone color (`CATEGORY_TONE`: Q→info, D→warn, I→err, C→idle) + the fixed Q→D→I→C order in `chipCategories`. A subtle vertical separator (1 px tall × 22% top/bottom inset, 28 % opacity of `currentColor`) sits between `+` and `−` via a `.chip .chip-add + .chip-sub::before` CSS rule — universal so the same separator also appears on `TlPhaseHeadChips` (which keeps its full bubble + value + add + sub presentation; only card chips slim down). `dim` (no-activity), `trailingSuffix` (capped `⊘ N`), `tone`, click handler, and `aria-label` are all preserved.
  - **Chip auto-dot collision safety net.** An audit at spec time surfaced four raw `<span className="chip tone-X">` call sites that bypass the `<Chip>` primitive (`compare.jsx:217-292`, `run-detail.jsx:5003`, `how-it-works.jsx:861`/`885`/`1006`) and therefore don't get the JSX-side `.no-dot` auto-application that suppresses the `::before` auto-dot when a slot is used. New CSS rule mirrors the JSX heuristic via `:has()` — `.chip:has(.chip-label)::before` / `.chip-value` / `.chip-dot` / `.cat-bubble` / `.chip-leading-icon` set `display: none`. Result: zero double-dots anywhere on the run-detail surface.
  - **Files touched.** `src/dual_research/ui/static/run-detail.jsx` (TimelineAgentPill + render site + PhaseDots + TlTurnRow chip call site + TimelineAgentBar removal), `src/dual_research/ui/static/shared.jsx` (AgentStrip costFormatter prop), `src/dual_research/ui/static/components.css` (`.as.in-header` modifier + narrow media block extension + `.phase-progress` + `.chip-add + .chip-sub` separator + `:has()` safety net), `src/dual_research/ui/static/index.html` (cache-bust `?v=0133b → ?v=0133c` on 25 `<link>` / `<script>` tags), `pyproject.toml` + `src/dual_research/__init__.py` (`1.6.12 → 1.7.0` minor bump), `uv.lock` (refresh).
  - **`.as.as-timeline` becomes dead code.** With `<TimelineAgentBar>` removed, the `.as.as-timeline` rule at `components.css:283-295` has no callers. Deliberately left in place for a future cleanup spec (keeps this diff focused on the new placement).

### Changed

- **Spec 0134 — JSX `fontWeight` bypass cleanup** ([spec 0134](specs/0134-fontweight-token-fixes.md)). Post-0132 audit caught **57 JSX call sites hardcoding `fontWeight: NNN`** (raw 400/500/600/700 literals) instead of consuming `var(--md-w-*)`. (First sweep landed 33 sites; an audit-grep bug — `grep -v "var("` excluded any line that referenced a token anywhere — hid the other 24, which were inline-mixed with `color: 'var(--md-on-surface)'` etc. Second sweep on the same PR caught all 24.) The numbers happened to match the M3 token values so visuals were unchanged, but the elements were not actually subscribed to the design system — the migration's intent was that every styled element reads from tokens, and these 33 sites silently bypassed that. Same shape as the v1-token consumption gap that the 0127→0131 arc closed in CSS, just in JSX inline-style props.
  - **Sweep mapping.** `400` → `var(--md-w-regular)`. `500` → `var(--md-w-medium)`. `600` → `var(--md-w-semi)`. `700` → `var(--md-w-bold)`.
  - **Per-file scope.** `app.jsx` (1 × 600). `auth.jsx` (2 × 600, 1 × 500). `design-language.jsx` (3 × 500). `errors.jsx` (1 × 600, 2 × 500). `run-list.jsx` (3 × 600). `run-detail.jsx` (11 × 600, 4 × 500, 2 × 700, plus the `current ? 700 : 600` ternary at the phase-header band). `settings.jsx` (2 × 600). `shared.jsx` (1 × 600). Plus the agent-tint gradient overlays in `auth.jsx:163, 208`: `rgba(124, 196, 184, 0.06)` (sage / agent-b) and `rgba(212, 165, 116, 0.06)` (sable / agent-a) replaced with `color-mix(in srgb, var(--md-secondary) 6%, transparent)` and `color-mix(in srgb, var(--md-primary) 6%, transparent)` so the auth-screen background gradients also subscribe to the M3 palette. The `rgba(0,0,0,0.10)` button shadow on `auth.jsx:182` was kept — that's a shadow-recipe pattern (same as `--md-elev-*` definitions in `tokens.css`).
  - **What didn't move and why.** No `fontWeight` bypass also reads from the design system today — the hardcoded numbers already match the token values, so the production CSSOM produces identical computed weights pre- and post-sweep. The value of the change is structural: if `--md-w-semi` ever moves to 650 (or similar), all 21 elements that read it will move with it. Pre-sweep, only 21 elements would have moved.
  - **Acceptance grep.** `grep -rnE "fontWeight:\s*[0-9]+\b" src/dual_research/ui/static/*.jsx | grep -v "var("` returns **0**.
  - **Version bump 1.6.10 → 1.6.11** (PATCH — refactoring). Cache-bust `?v=0132a → ?v=0133a` on every `<link>` / `<script>` in `index.html` (25 occurrences).

### Removed

- **Spec 0132 — Design-system pollution scrub (post-arc cleanup)** ([spec 0132](specs/0132-design-system-pollution-scrub.md)). Follow-up to the 0127 → 0131 v1 → v2 arc. Targets residue the arc didn't scope: a 3-site `var(--w-semibold)` typo silently under-weighting labels at 400 instead of 600; the duplicate `--w-*` weight vocabulary (51 refs identical to `--md-w-*`); a stale `V1 Component vocabulary` banner on `components.css`; ~18 stale v1/migration narrative comments scattered across the four CSS files; the dead `.chip-pill` class (pill is the default since spec 0119); 4 `border-radius: 999px` literals that should consume `var(--md-shape-full)`; the dead `.chip.tone-a` / `.chip.tone-b` aliases (zero JSX consumers); and the dormant `specs/NNNN-run-detail-header-rework.md` draft (carried untracked across 4 specs). Spacing vocab (`--s-*` ↔ `--md-sp-*`) and elevation/motion/easing left for separate decisions — they're not in pollution territory.
  - **Phase 1 — `var(--w-semibold)` typo fix.** Three JSX call sites (`compare.jsx:91`, `search.jsx:71`, `shortcuts-overlay.jsx:44`) referenced an undefined token, silently rendering at the CSS default `400`. Replaced with `var(--md-w-semi)` (600). Quiet under-weighting fixed on the "Compare runs" page title, "Cross-run search" page title, and shortcuts-overlay group headers.
  - **Phase 2 — weight vocab consolidation.** `--w-regular` / `--w-medium` / `--w-semi` / `--w-bold` were duplicates of `--md-w-regular/medium/semi/bold` (same 400 / 500 / 600 / 700 values). 51 refs in `base.css` + `components.css` migrated to the `--md-w-*` family. The `--w-*` definition block (lines 72-75) deleted from `tokens.css`. Reverses the 0131 handover's "kept --w-*" decision: that was a description of state at the time, not a permanent design intent. One token vocabulary in code (`--md-*` everywhere) is the v2 principle.
  - **Phase 3 — stale narrative comment scrub.** ~20 comments rewritten across `tokens.css`, `base.css`, `components.css`, `theme.css` to drop "v1 → v2 migration completed", "additive — no collision with v1", "subsequent specs migrate", "Code is being migrated to X; until then both work", "Mirrors v2-m3.css... Additive — v1 classes above remain", "the source of the v1/M3 split", and similar transitional narrative. Most visibly: the `components.css:2` banner changed from `dual-research — V1 Component vocabulary` to `dual-research — Component vocabulary (Material 3)`. Spec-attribution lines that describe design intent (e.g. `Spec 0111 — single source of truth for list-item card padding`) preserved.
  - **Phase 4 — `.chip-pill` dead-code removal.** `.chip` already defaults to `border-radius: var(--md-shape-full)`; `.chip-pill` was a redundant no-op that the previous comment flagged as "legacy" and `shared.jsx` flagged as a `// legacy no-op (pill is default)`. Deleted from `run-detail.jsx:5001` (the only real consumer), `shared.jsx:780` (the legacy alias in the `clsx` array), and `components.css:132-135` (the comment + rule). The stale "Legacy slot — kept for back-compat with `<Chip icon=…>`" comment at `components.css:239` rewritten to describe the live slot accurately (5 active consumers). Also deleted: dead `.chip.tone-a` / `.chip.tone-b` alias rules (0 JSX consumers) + their `Legacy tone names — alias to the canonical pair. Code is being migrated to tone-claude / tone-gpt; until then both work.` comment.
  - **Phase 5 — `999px` literals tokenized.** 4 `border-radius: 999px` in `components.css` (lines for `.cap-pill`, `.settings-input--search`, `.kf-pill`, `.search-pill`) → `var(--md-shape-full)`. Completes the convention the 0131 theme drain established for `.dr-section-brief-btn` and `.cap-bar`. No visual change (every affected element is < 100 px tall; `999` vs `9999` resolves identically).
  - **Phase 6 — `specs/NNNN-run-detail-header-rework.md` filed as 0133.** The 513-line draft carried untracked across the 0127→0131 arc was renamed to `specs/0133-run-detail-header-rework.md`, frontmatter `spec:` updated to `0133`, and a `status: proposed` banner added. Filed, not implemented; awaiting greenlight.
  - **Version bump 1.6.9 → 1.6.10** (PATCH — refactoring). Cache-bust `?v=0131a → ?v=0132a` on every `<link>` / `<script>` in `index.html` (25 occurrences).

- **Spec 0131 — CSS finalization + v1 token block removal + IBM Plex removal — closes the v1 → v2 migration arc** ([spec 0131](specs/0131-css-finalization-v1-removal.md)). **Fifth and final spec in the 5-spec migration arc (0127 → 0131)**, and the **no-return commit**. After this PR every `var(--bg-*)` / `var(--fg-*)` / `var(--border-*)` / `var(--r-*)` / `var(--t-*)` / `var(--mono)` / `var(--sans)` / `var(--serif)` reference anywhere in the codebase resolves to undefined and breaks visibly — no silent fallback masks a missed migration. The IBM Plex `<link>` tags are gone from `index.html`; the only fonts loaded are Roboto Flex + Roboto Serif + Material Symbols Outlined. `design-system/SPEC.md § 12 Migration status` is deleted (formal "migration is done" signal).
  - **CSS sweep — three commits, 458 references replaced.** `base.css` (43 v1 + 10 font aliases), `components.css` (383 v1 + 33 font aliases + 10 `--r-pill` + 1 orphan `--t-sm`), `theme.css` (15 v1 + 2 `--mono`). Same mechanical mapping as 0128 / 0129 / 0130, extended with three new entries surfaced at audit time: `var(--r-pill)` → `var(--md-shape-full)`, `var(--r-4)` → unused (definition-only deletion), `var(--t-sm)` → `var(--md-body-s-size)` (the v1 token was never defined; this swap also fixes a pre-existing inherit-from-parent bug in `.quote-callout`).
  - **`theme.css` legacy-class drain.** Six legacy class families had their dispositions decided at audit time via grep of every JSX consumer: `.phase-step-line` and `.bg-grid` had no remaining consumers → DELETE. `.uppercase-label` (4 sites in design-language.jsx), `.dr-ghost-block` family (run-detail.jsx), `.dr-section-brief-btn` (run-detail.jsx), `.cap-bar` family (design-language.jsx) → MOVED to a new "Spec 0131 — legacy classes drained from theme.css" section at the end of `components.css`. Two literal `border-radius: 999px` declarations converted to `var(--md-shape-full)` on the way. `theme.css` shrinks from 144 → 41 lines, now containing only the M3 `body.tint-secondary` + `body.compact` hooks.
  - **JSX residual sweep — 17 refs across 5 files** caught at the pre-deletion gate. 0130's handover flagged `run-list.jsx` (2 mono) and `search-palette.jsx` (5 mono) as residuals; 0131's wider re-audit additionally turned up `search-palette.jsx` (1 sans), `search.jsx` (1 sans), `shared.jsx` (2 sans), and `design-language.jsx` (6 sans + 1 serif). All swept to `--md-font-plain` / `--md-font-brand` / `--md-font-data` before the v1 token block deletion landed, so no consumer falls back to browser default fonts post-deploy.
  - **`tokens.css` v1 block deletion — the no-return commit.** Removed: 5 `--bg-*` defs, 5 `--fg-*` defs, 3 `--border-*` defs, 5 `--r-*` defs, 7 `--t-*` defs, 3 font-alias defs (`--sans`/`--serif`/`--mono`), plus the 15 v1 light-mode overrides in `body.light, .light`. Kept intact: agent identity (`--agent-a/b`), status hues (`--ok`/`--info`/`--warn`/`--err`/`--idle`), `--on-accent`, `--lh-*`, `--w-*`, `--s-*`, `--bw-*`, `--e-*`, `--m-*`, `--ease`, `--focus-ring`, `--chrome-h`, `--content-max`, `--consumption-*`, and the entire `--md-*` M3 block. Header comment rewritten to read "Design System v2 (Material 3) — Tokens".
  - **IBM Plex `<link>` removal.** Deleted from `index.html` along with the stale SPEC-0092 comment about "v1 body keeps IBM Plex". `grep -i "IBM Plex" src/dual_research/ui/static/index.html` returns 0.
  - **FullReference SwatchGrid + border narration rewrite (deferred from 0129).** `design-language.jsx:451-472` — 14 SwatchGrid items rewritten with v2 short-names + v2-resolved hex values. Surfaces: `bg-0..4` → `surface` / `surf-low` / `surf-mid` / `surf-high` / `surf-highest`. Foreground: `fg-0..4` → `on-surface` / `on-variant` / `on-muted` / `on-faint` / `on-decor` (hexes shifted by 8-25 percent against the live tokens — the FullReference was documenting a stale palette before this commit). Status: names + hexes already matched `tokens.css`, no change needed. Border narration line at `:587` rewrote from "border-1/2/3 hairline/medium/strong" to "outline-hair/variant/outline" with the resolved hex (#1c1f24) verified against `--md-outline-hair`. `/#/language?full=1` now documents v2.
  - **`design-system/SPEC.md § 12 Migration status` removed.** Old § 13 ("Open items") renumbered to § 12. Downstream § 12 references in `design-system/CHANGELOG.md:27` (rephrased to past-tense) and `design-system/PROMPT-FOR-CLAUDE-DESIGN.md:107` (rewritten to the v2-canonical post-arc statement) updated to match.
  - **Version bump 1.6.8 → 1.6.9** (PATCH — refactoring). Cache-bust `?v=0130a → ?v=0131a` on every `<link>` / `<script>` in `index.html` (25 occurrences) so browsers fetch the v2-only bundle.

### Changed

- **Spec 0130 — Remaining JSX (`app/errors/compare/auth/search/shared.jsx`) v1 → v2 token migration** ([spec 0130](specs/0130-remaining-jsx-m3-migration.md)). Fourth spec in the 5-spec v1 → v2 design-system migration arc (0127 → 0131); **third live-code spec**. Sweeps every v1 surface / ink / outline / shape / font reference inside the six remaining JSX files in `src/dual_research/ui/static/` (the last block of v1 token usage in JSX) over to its `--md-*` equivalent. 207 token replacements across 6 files via 6 sequenced commits (one per file), plus 4 `--t-*` type-token resolutions inlined per § 5b. No markup or logic changes. After this PR, **every `.jsx` file in the static directory is on the v2 token vocabulary**; v1 surface/ink/outline/shape/mono references survive only inside CSS files until spec 0131 cleans those up.
  - **Per-file scope.** `errors.jsx` (40 refs), `app.jsx` (43), `compare.jsx` (42), `auth.jsx` (36), `search.jsx` (27), `shared.jsx` (19). Per-file order: errors → auth → search → compare → app → shared, so the lowest-blast-radius files land first and the design-system primitives in `shared.jsx` (`<Chip>`, `<Card>`, `<Tab>`, `<TabGroup>`, `<AgentStrip>`, `<StatusBadge>`, `<CollapsibleSection>`, `<QuoteCallout>`, `<LoadingState>`, `<BrandMark>`, `<ModalDialog>`) get swept last with a smaller per-file scope. Each file shipped as a separate logical commit so reverts can be surgical.
  - **Token mapping applied.** Same as 0128 / 0129. `--bg-0/1/2/3` → `--md-surface` / `--md-surface-container-low` / `--md-surface-container` / `--md-surface-container-high`. `--fg-0/1/2/3/4` → `--md-on-surface` / `--md-on-surface-variant` / `--md-on-surface-muted` / `--md-on-surface-faint` / `--md-on-surface-decor`. `--border-1/2/3` → `--md-outline-hair` / `--md-outline-variant` / `--md-outline`. `--r-1/2/3` → `--md-shape-xs` / `--md-shape-sm` / `--md-shape-md`. `var(--mono)` → `var(--md-font-data)` (Roboto Flex + tabular-nums; the v1 `--mono` was an IBM Plex Sans alias scheduled for deletion in spec 0131, so the 20 JSX consumption sites are pre-empted here to avoid a follow-up sweep).
  - **Type-token resolutions (4 call sites, all inline `fontSize:`).** `compare.jsx:89` (chrome-bar "Compare runs" span) `--t-title` (20 px) → `--md-title-l` (22 / 28, +2 px). `search.jsx:69` (page h2 "Cross-run search") same swap, +2 px. `search.jsx:78` (subtitle p) `--t-body` (13 px) → `--md-body-m` (14 / 20, +1 px). `shared.jsx:930` (`<AgentStrip>` model-ID span) `--t-mono` (11 px) → `--md-label-s` (11 / 16, exact size match; M3 `--md-label-s-track` letter-spacing skipped because the call site is a model-ID string, not a label). Each adds a paired `lineHeight: 'var(--md-<role>-lh)'` so the M3 line-height comes through.
  - **Minor visual deltas (documented, expected).** `--r-2` (6 px) → `--md-shape-sm` (8 px) and `--r-3` (8 px) → `--md-shape-md` (12 px) bump radii by +2–4 px across the six surfaces. Same shape convergence applied to run-detail (0128), design-language (0129), and run-list (0108) — the live JSX continues to converge on the canonical shape scale in `design-system/SPEC.md § 2.6`. The 20 `var(--mono)` sites swap IBM Plex Sans for Roboto Flex with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` baked in. `compare.jsx` row-pill radii bump may shift table column widths by 1–2 px.
  - **Untouched.** No CSS edits (the v1 token block stays in `tokens.css` until spec 0131). No edits to `run-detail.jsx` / `design-language.jsx` (already migrated). No markup, no logic, no other prop changes. Agent identity tokens (`--agent-a/b`, `--claude`, `--gpt`) and status hues (`--ok`, `--info`, `--warn`, `--err`, `--idle` + their `-bg` / `-border` variants) are M3-orthogonal and stay as-is. No literal v1 token names as display content found (wider grep clean — unlike 0129's DnaSwatch palette case).
  - **Flagged for follow-up.** `var(--w-semibold)` is referenced at `compare.jsx:90`, `search.jsx:70`, and `shortcuts-overlay.jsx:43` but never defined in `tokens.css` (only `--w-regular / --w-medium / --w-semi / --w-bold` exist) — currently resolves to default weight 400 instead of the intended 600. Pre-existing quiet visual bug; out of scope for this arc (the `--w-*` family lives outside `--bg/fg/border/r/t/mono` removal). Worth a small follow-up spec.
  - **Acceptance grep clean.** `grep -clE 'var\(--(bg|fg|border|r|t)-' src/dual_research/ui/static/{app,errors,compare,auth,search,shared}.jsx` → 0 files. `grep -clE 'var\(--mono' …` → 0 files. Full pytest suite stays green.
  - **Version bump 1.6.7 → 1.6.8** (PATCH — refactoring). Cache-bust `?v=0129a → ?v=0130a` on every `<link>` / `<script>` in `index.html` (25 occurrences) so browsers fetch the new JSX bundles.

- **Spec 0129 — `design-language.jsx` v1 → v2 token migration** ([spec 0129](specs/0129-design-language-jsx-m3-migration.md)). Third spec in the 5-spec v1 → v2 design-system migration arc (0127 → 0131); **second live-code spec**. Sweeps every v1 surface / ink / outline / shape / font reference inside `src/dual_research/ui/static/design-language.jsx` (the in-app design-system reference page at `/#/language`, ~965 lines) over to its `--md-*` equivalent. 179 token replacements via 14 mechanical find/replaces, plus an 8-swatch relabel on the DNA palette so the labels match the new tokens. No markup or logic changes. After this PR, the in-app design-language page reads from the v2 (M3) vocabulary end-to-end on every surface the mechanical sweep can reach.
  - **Token mapping applied.** `--bg-0/1/2/3` → `--md-surface` / `--md-surface-container-low` / `--md-surface-container` / `--md-surface-container-high`. `--fg-0/1/2/3` → `--md-on-surface` / `--md-on-surface-variant` / `--md-on-surface-muted` / `--md-on-surface-faint`. `--border-1/2/3` → `--md-outline-hair` / `--md-outline-variant` / `--md-outline`. `--r-2/3` → `--md-shape-sm` / `--md-shape-md`. `var(--mono)` → `var(--md-font-data)` (Roboto Flex + tabular-nums; the v1 `--mono` was an IBM Plex Sans alias scheduled for deletion in spec 0131, so the 6 JSX consumption sites are pre-empted here to avoid a follow-up sweep). The unused-in-this-file rows the original draft listed (`--fg-4`, `--r-1`, `--bg-4`, `--t-*` type tokens) were dropped after the re-audit returned zero references.
  - **DNA palette relabel.** The 8 swatches in the DNA one-pager palette (`design-language.jsx:48-61`) had v1 token short-names baked into their `label=` / `token=` display props (`label="bg-0"` / `token="--bg-0"`, etc.). Without a relabel, a pure mechanical sweep would render the v2 surface color under a label that still read "bg-0" — the page would actively misrepresent the palette. Swatches now read `surface` / `surf-low` / `surf-mid` / `surf-high` / `on-surface` / `on-variant` / `on-muted` / `on-faint`, with `token=` carrying the full canonical M3 token name (`--md-surface`, etc.) so users can copy it out.
  - **Minor visual deltas (documented, expected).** `--r-2` (6 px) → `--md-shape-sm` (8 px) and `--r-3` (8 px) → `--md-shape-md` (12 px) bump radii by +2–4 px on every spotlight card. Same shape convergence applied to run-detail (0128) and run-list (0108) — the file converges to the canonical shape scale in `design-system/SPEC.md § 2.6`. The 6 `var(--mono)` sites swap IBM Plex Sans for Roboto Flex with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` baked in.
  - **Untouched.** No CSS edits (the v1 token block stays in `tokens.css` until spec 0131). No other JSX (`app/errors/compare/auth/search/shared.jsx` is spec 0130). No markup, no logic, no other prop changes. Agent identity tokens (`--agent-a/b`, `--claude`, `--gpt`) and status hues (`--ok`, `--info`, `--warn`, `--err`, `--idle` + their `-bg` / `-border` variants) are M3-orthogonal and stay as-is.
  - **Deferred to follow-up.** The FullReference `SwatchGrid` items at `design-language.jsx:451-465` (13 swatches with literal v1 names + baked-in v1 dark-mode hexes + role text) and the inline narration string at `:587` (`"border-1 (#1c1f24) hairline · …"`) stay v1 in this spec — they need v2 hex re-baselining and editorial rewriting that doesn't belong in a mechanical sweep. Tracked as a follow-up that naturally rides spec 0131 (v1 token block removal forces re-baselining anyway).
  - **Acceptance grep clean.** `grep -cE 'var\(--(bg|fg|border|r|t)-' design-language.jsx` → 0. `grep -cE 'var\(--mono' design-language.jsx` → 0. `grep -oE 'var\(--md-[a-z0-9-]+\)' design-language.jsx | wc -l` → 179 (up from 0 baseline). Full pytest suite stays green.
  - **Version bump 1.6.6 → 1.6.7** (PATCH — refactoring). Cache-bust `?v=0128a → ?v=0129a` on every `<link>` / `<script>` in `index.html` (25 occurrences) so browsers fetch the new JSX bundle.

- **Spec 0127 — Design system v2 canonicalization** ([spec 0127](specs/0127-design-system-v2-canonicalization.md)). Promotes the v2 (Material 3) design system to single source of truth and retires the parallel v1 / v2 split that had accumulated. The previous `design-system/SPEC.md` (a v1 text reference) is archived; the new `SPEC.md` is rewritten end-to-end as v2-canonical. The `docs/design-system-v2/` briefing folder is dissolved — its contents move into `design-system/` (assets + notion-issues) or `design-system/_archive/seeding/` (the historical briefing + Claude Code prompt). CSS source-of-truth files are renamed to drop the briefing-phase prefix: `v2-m3.css` → `tokens-and-primitives.css`; `v2-m3-page.css` → `composed-components.css`. The `PROMPT-FOR-CLAUDE-DESIGN.md` is updated to v2 vocabulary (Roboto Flex + Roboto Serif type system, `--md-*` token examples, Material Symbols Outlined for icons, M3 elevation + state-layer + motion language) so new design proposals are framed against v2 from the start.
  - **Folder restructure.** New layout: `design-system/{SPEC.md, README.md, PROMPT-FOR-CLAUDE-DESIGN.md, CHANGELOG.md, assets/, notion-issues/, audits/, skills/, _archive/{v1, seeding}}`. The `docs/design-system-v2/` folder is deleted entirely; readers who had it bookmarked find the contents one level up under `design-system/`.
  - **No live frontend code change.** `tokens.css`, `base.css`, `components.css`, `theme.css`, every `*.jsx`, and `index.html` are untouched in this spec. The live frontend continues to run on the additive layering pattern that spec 0092 introduced (v1 and v2 token vocabularies coexist in the live CSS). The live-code v2 migration is deferred to a 4-spec arc (0128–0131) tracked in `design-system/SPEC.md § 12 Migration status`; that section is removed when 0131 lands.
  - **Version bumps converge.** This commit catches up a pre-existing pyproject ↔ `__init__` drift: `pyproject.toml` jumps `1.5.0 → 1.6.5` (skipping 1.6.0–1.6.4 which had only been bumped in `__init__.py` during hotfixes); `__init__.py` goes `1.6.4 → 1.6.5`. Both files agree at 1.6.5 going forward. Cache-bust unchanged (no CSS/JS loaded by `index.html` changed) — stays at `?v=0126a`.

### Fixed

- **Spec 0126 — Changelog: open-spec full-view modal + drop broken screenshot paths** ([spec 0126](specs/0126-changelog-spec-modal-and-working-screenshots.md)). The Changelog tab inside the How-It-Works overlay had two visible problems on the live site: (a) every "Open spec ↗" button opened a new tab to `/specs/<id>.md`, which 404'd because the `specs/` directory is at the repo root and isn't served by the static UI mount; (b) every entry's `screenshots[]` referenced PNGs at `/changelog-shots/<version>/<file>.png` that were never captured and 404'd on every render (9 broken `<img>` tags in total). This spec lands a server-rendered spec endpoint + a full-view spec modal that mirrors the timeline-card modal pattern, and removes all broken screenshot references.
  - **New `GET /api/specs/{spec_id}`** in `server.py` (both fs and supabase modes). Returns the spec markdown body with YAML frontmatter stripped. Regex-gated id (`^[0-9a-zA-Z][0-9a-zA-Z\-_]*$`) refuses path traversal with 400; missing spec returns 404. Supabase-mode requires authentication. 14 new tests in `tests/ui/test_specs_api.py` cover the lifecycle (exact + prefix match, 404, regex rejection, content-type, frontmatter stripping, auth gating).
  - **New `<SpecModal>` component** in `how-it-works.jsx`. Wraps the shared `<Modal variant="rich">` — the SAME M3 dialog primitive (spec 0096) the timeline-card full-view modals use. Same chrome: ESC to close, scrim click closes, focus trap, body overflow lock. Title shows `Spec NNNN`; subtitle shows `v<version> · <date> · <summary>`. Body fetches from `/api/specs/<id>`, renders via the existing `<Markdown>` component (same renderer the timeline turn bodies use), and scrolls internally inside `max-height: 75vh`. Includes loading + error states.
  - **ChangelogEntry button handler swap.** The pre-0126 `<button onClick={() => window.open(entry.specPath, '_blank')}>Open spec ↗</button>` is replaced by a per-spec button that mounts `<SpecModal>` via state owned by `ChangelogList`. Each entry can list multiple specs (the data shape already supported it); buttons render as `Open spec NNNN ↗` one per spec.
  - **Screenshot cleanup.** All four affected entries (v1.5.0, v1.4.1, v1.4.0, v1.3.0) have their `screenshots: [...]` arrays cleared to `[]`. The 9 broken `<img>` tags are gone. The `screenshots[]` field stays in the data shape for future entries with verified images. Added an `onError` handler on the screenshot `<img>` so any future 404 hides the figure instead of rendering a broken-image icon.
  - **VERSION_NOTES backfill.** New v1.6.0 entry (the spec-0125 release) prepended to the array with the full prose. `screenshots: []` (the spec modal carries the content). The `specPath` field is dropped from all entries; `specs: [...]` is the single source of truth.
  - **CSS additions** (~85 lines under a spec-0126 block in `components.css`): `.spec-modal__body` (max-height + overflow), `.spec-modal__error`, plus internal element styling for headings / tables / code / pre / blockquote within the modal body.
  - **Version bump 1.6.1 → 1.6.2** (PATCH — bug fix to the same surface, additive endpoint). Cache-bust `?v=0125b → ?v=0126a`.

### Added

- **Spec 0125 — Onboarding tour visual rewrite + unified Admin/Settings + server-persisted onboarding state** ([spec 0125](specs/0125-onboarding-rewrite-and-unified-admin-settings.md)). Three full-stack deliverables in one PR (frontend + backend + Supabase migration).
  - **Tour visuals.** The flat-dim, no-cutout, no-halo experience that 5 of the 8 spotlight steps fell into is replaced with an SVG-based `<TourSpotlight>` — `<mask>` cutout around the anchor + radial-gradient halo + crisp info-toned outline ring. Callout card has M3 elevation-2 + info-border + CSS arrow pointing at the anchor. Card placement reuses the overflow-aware positioner from spec 0121 (right → below → left → above with viewport clamp).
  - **Skip-on-missing-anchor.** If the anchor isn't on the page after 4×250 ms retries (~1 s), the step is auto-skipped with a bottom-right toast `"Step N skipped — no <anchor> on this page."` The tour advances; users are never stuck on a centered card with nothing to point at.
  - **Cross-route navigation.** Spotlight steps 3, 5, 6, 7 (all anchor inside a run-detail page) navigate to the most-recent run before they fire. If no run exists, the skip path quietly handles it.
  - **Phase vocabulary refresh.** Step 4's inlined `PHASES_SVG` is replaced with the spec-0121 `/diagrams/how-it-works/01-pipeline.{light,dark}.svg` (theme-aware). Step 4 body + step 5 body updated to the spec-0114 vocabulary (Input / Research plan / Negotiate plan / Draft / Review draft / Finalize).
  - **Unified Admin/Settings.** `/admin/users` is folded into `/settings` as a sub-tab. New `SettingsScreen` renders a Settings page with tab strip `[Allowlist] [Users]`, hash-routed (`#/settings#allowlist` default, `#/settings#users`). The `admin-users.jsx` file is **deleted**; the avatar menu drops "Admin: users". Router redirects `/admin/users` → `/settings`.
  - **Users sub-tab.** Real multi-user table backed by the new `/api/users` endpoint. Columns: select-checkbox · email · role · onboarding status (`✓ completed · v1.5.0` / `in progress · step 5/8` / `⊘ not started` / `reset pending`) · last seen (relative) · actions (`Reset onboarding`, `Remove`). Filter chips `[All N] [Admins N] [Pending N] [Completed N]` + search input. Bulk-action bar appears when ≥1 row is checked. Global onboarding control panel: `[✓] Required for all new sign-ins` toggle + `[Reset for everyone]` button.
  - **Server-persisted onboarding state.** New Supabase migration `0005_onboarding_state.sql` adds five columns to `approved_emails` (`onboarded_at`, `onboarded_at_version`, `tour_step`, `tour_force_reset_at`, `last_seen_at`) + a `system_settings` table with `onboarding_required` flag. New endpoints: `GET /api/users` (admin), `POST /api/users/<email>/reset-onboarding` (admin), `POST /api/users/bulk-reset-onboarding` (admin), `POST /api/onboarding/broadcast-reset` (admin), `GET /api/onboarding/state` (authed), `PUT /api/onboarding/state` (authed), `GET /api/system-settings` (authed), `PUT /api/system-settings` (admin). Server-side `mustRestart` rule: `tour_force_reset_at IS NOT NULL AND (onboarded_at IS NULL OR tour_force_reset_at > onboarded_at)`. `/api/me` now bumps `last_seen_at` on every call.
  - **One-shot localStorage migration.** On first authed boot per browser, the tour module checks for the legacy `dr_onboarded === 'true'` flag and `PUT`s `onboardedAt = now()` to the server, then clears localStorage. Subsequent boots use server state.
  - **Defensive against missing migration.** Every new endpoint catches schema errors and falls back to legacy columns (or returns sensible defaults) so the deploy + the Supabase migration can land in either order without 500s.
  - **22 new backend tests** in `tests/ui/test_server_users_api.py` covering admin gating, bulk operations, broadcast reset, `mustRestart` ordering, `system_settings` round-trip, and the `last_seen_at` bump.
  - **CSS** (~314 lines under a spec-0125 block): `.tour-overlay`, `.tour-scrim`, `.tour-spotlight*`, `.tour-callout` family + 4 arrow directions, `.tour-skip-toast`, `.settings-page*`, `.settings-tabs`, `.settings-table`, `.settings-btn*`, `.settings-input*`, `.settings-add-row`, `.global-onboarding-panel`, `.gop__*`, `.users-filter-row`, `.users-chip*`, `.users-bulk-bar*`, `.users-table-wrap`, `.users-you`, `.users-status--{ok,info,warn,muted}`.
  - **Version bump 1.5.2 → 1.6.0** (MINOR — new admin functionality + schema migration + new endpoints + onboarding UX overhaul). Cache-bust `?v=0124a → ?v=0125a`.

After: a stable Q/D/I/C/✓ stack hangs off the right edge of every card, identical column-to-column. Measured `gapToEdge: 0 px` on every card in the test run.

Two narrow UX changes on top of the spec-0121 surface: (a) the How-It-Works overlay is no longer a modal — it's a full-page route at `#/how-it-works` that uses up to `--md-content-max` (1440 px) of horizontal space, the same layout pattern as Design Language / Settings / Compare / Search; (b) every embedded `<HiwDiagram>` SVG is now a click-to-enlarge button that opens a viewer modal showing the SVG at `max(95 vw, 90 vh)` with `object-fit: contain`. Document title reflects the active tab (`How it works · Dual Research` / `Changelog · Dual Research`). Side menu collapses to a stacked layout on viewports under 900 px. The pre-spec-0123 `<HowItWorks>` modal-mode component (and its `window.HowItWorks` global, the `howOpen` / `openHow` / `closeHow` state in `app.jsx`, the `onOpenHow` prop plumbed through `ChromeBar` → `RightCluster`, and the `?how=1` deep-link query param) are all retired.

No other trigger sites referenced the modal — confirmed via `git grep` against the static directory.

Click → renders the viewer over a `92%` cream/dark scrim with the SVG at `max-width: 95 vw, max-height: 90 vh, object-fit: contain`. Esc / scrim-click / × button all close. Theme-aware: viewer reads the same `useThemeMode` hook so it shows the right variant.
